Since entries after the found one need prev key. So when advancing, keep prev key. Implementation: iter from restart; loop { save pos/key; decode; if key >= target break with iter state = (offset=entry_pos, key=prev_key) }. BlockIter stores `next_offset` and `prev_key`. So:\n\n```rust\npub struct BlockIter { block: BlockReader, next_offset: usize, prev_key: Vec<u8>, done: bool }\nfn next(): if done or next_offset >= entries_end → None. decode at next_offset. reconstruct key = prev_key[..shared] + delta. update prev_key = key.clone(), next_offset = entry_end. return (key, value).\n```\n\nseek returns iter with next_offset = position of first entry >= target and prev_key = key before it. \n\nFor scan with start bound: seek(start) in start block. For subsequent blocks: iter from 0.\n\nTableReader::scan_range lazy iterator:\n\n```rust\npub struct ScanIter<'a, R> { reader: &'a TableReader<R>, end: Bound-ish Vec<u8> + has_end bool, next_block: usize, cur: Option<BlockIter>, finished: bool }\nfn next(): loop { if let Some(it) = cur { match it.next() { Some(Ok(kv)) => if within end → return, else finished; Some(Err) → return err; None → cur=None } } else { if next_block >= index.len() → None; load block next_block (parse+crc); cur = Some(if first block {seek(start)} else {iter()}); next_block+=1 } }\n```\n\nError type in iterator item: Result<(Vec<u8>,Vec<u8>)>.\n\nget(): \n```\nlet i = index.partition_point(|(sep,_,_)| sep.as_slice() < key);\nif i == len → None\nblock = read_block(i)\niter = block.seek(key)\nfor item in iter { let (k,v) = item?; if k == key return Some(v); if k > key return None }\nNone\n```\n\nread_block(i): read size bytes at offset via ReadAt, BlockReader::parse.\n\nverify(): as designed. Also verify index block itself strictly (BlockReader::verify_strict) and index separators strictly increasing (verify_strict on index block checks key ordering already). Check index value decode: offset+size within file bounds (<= index_offset). Check blocks contiguous: expected_offset accumulates from 0; each index entry offset == expected; expected += size; final == index_offset. Check separator == last key of block. Count entries == footer num_entries.\n\nNow the writer flush: block_size check — flush when estimated_size >= block_size and block non-empty, checked before adding next entry? Standard: after add, if estimated >= block_size, flush. Fine.\n\nEdge: single very large entry → block bigger than block_size; fine.\n\nEmpty table: no data blocks; index block empty (BlockBuilder with no entries: restarts=[0], buf empty → finish gives restart array [0], num=1, crc — an \"empty\" block with zero entries but one restart offset pointing at 0 where entries_end=0). BlockReader::parse: entries_end=0, iter → None immediately. verify_strict: restarts[0]==0 ok, entry offsets set empty, restart 0 must be an entry start? With zero entries, offset 0 == entries_end. v >>= 7 } out.push(v as u8) }\npub fn get_uvarint(data: &[u8]) -> Option<(u64, usize)> { let mut v=0u64; let mut s=0; for (i,&b) in data.iter().enumerate() { if i == 10 { return None } if b < 0x80 { if i==9 && b > 1 { return None } return Some((v | (b as u64) << s, i+1)) } v |= ((b & 0x7f) as u64) << s; s += 7 } None }\n```\n\nblock.rs — write it.\n\n```rust\nuse crate::crc32::crc32;\nuse crate::error::{Error, Result};\nuse crate::varint::{get_uvarint, put_uvarint};\n\npub const BLOCK_CRC_SIZE: usize = 4;\nconst RESTART_ENTRY_SIZE: usize = 4;\n\npub struct BlockBuilder {\n    restart_interval: usize,\n    buf: Vec<u8>,\n    restarts: Vec<u32>,\n    counter: usize,\n    last_key: Vec<u8>,\n    num_entries: usize,\n}\n\nimpl BlockBuilder {\n    pub fn new(restart_interval: usize) -> Self {\n        Self { restart_interval: restart_interval.max(1), buf: Vec::new(), restarts: vec![0], counter: 0, last_key: Vec::new(), num_entries: 0 }\n    }\n    pub fn add(&mut self, key: &[u8], value: &[u8]) {\n        let shared = if self.counter < self.restart_interval {\n            common_prefix(&self.last_key, key)\n        } else {\n            self.restarts.push(self.buf.len() as u32);\n            self.counter = 0;\n            0\n        };\n        put_uvarint(&mut self.buf, shared as u64);\n        put_uvarint(&mut self.buf, (key.len() - shared) as u64);\n        put_uvarint(&mut self.buf, value.len() as u64);\n        self.buf.extend_from_slice(&key[shared..]);\n        self.buf.extend_from_slice(value);\n        self.last_key.clear(); self.last_key.extend_from_slice(key);\n        self.counter += 1; self.num_entries += 1;\n    }\n    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool { self.num_entries == 0 }\n    pub fn num_entries(&self) -> usize\n    pub fn last_key(&self) -> &[u8]  // valid if !is_empty\n    pub fn estimated_size(&self) -> usize { self.buf.len() + self.restarts.len()*4 + 4 + 4 }\n    pub fn finish(&self) -> Vec<u8> {\n        let mut out = self.buf.clone();\n        for r in &self.restarts { out.extend_from_slice(&r.to_le_bytes()) }\n        out.extend_from_slice(&(self.restarts.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());\n        let crc = crc32(&out);\n        out.extend_from_slice(&crc.to_le_bytes());\n        out\n    }\n    pub fn reset(&mut self) { self.buf.clear(); self.restarts.clear(); self.restarts.push(0); self.counter=0; self.last_key.clear(); self.num_entries=0; }\n}\n```\n\nBlockReader:\n\n```rust\npub struct BlockReader {\n    data: Vec<u8>,        // full block including crc\n    entries_end: usize,   // offset where restart array begins\n    num_restarts: usize,\n}\nimpl BlockReader {\n    pub fn parse(raw: &[u8]) -> Result<BlockReader> {\n        if raw.len() < 12 { return Err(corruption(\"block too small\")); }\n        let stored = u32::from_le_bytes(raw[raw.len()-4..]);\n        if crc32(&raw[..raw.len()-4]) != stored { Err(\"block crc mismatch\") }\n        let body = &raw[..raw.len()-4];\n        let num = u32::from_le_bytes(body[body.len()-4..]) as usize;\n        if num == 0 { Err(\"zero restarts\") }\n        let restarts_bytes = num.checked_mul(4).ok_or(...)?;\n        if body.len() < 4 + restarts_bytes { Err(\"restart array out of bounds\") }\n        let entries_end = body.len() - 4 - restarts_bytes;\n        Ok(...)\n    }\n    fn restart_offset(&self, i: usize) -> usize {\n        let off = self.entries_end + i*4;\n        u32 from data[off..off+4] as usize\n    }\n    fn decode_header(&self, off: usize) -> Result<(usize shared, usize unshared, usize vlen, usize hdr)> \n    fn key_at(&self, off: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>>  // requires shared==0\n    pub fn iter(&self) -> BlockIter { BlockIter { block: self.clone()?... } }\n```\n\nBlockIter owns BlockReader → BlockReader must be Clone (Vec<u8> clone, fine).\n\nseek:\n\n```rust\npub fn seek(&self, target: &[u8]) -> Result<BlockIter> {\n    // binary search restarts\n    let mut lo = 0usize; }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n    fn flush_block(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n        if self.block.is_empty() { return Ok(()) }\n        let bytes = self.block.finish();\n        // index entry: separator = last key of this block\n        let mut v = Vec::new(); put_uvarint(&mut v, self.offset); put_uvarint(&mut v, bytes.len() as u64);\n        let sep = self.block.last_key().to_vec();\n        self.index.add(&sep, &v);\n        self.sink.write_all(&bytes)?;\n        self.offset += bytes.len() as u64;\n        self.num_blocks += 1;\n        self.block.reset();\n        Ok(())\n    }\n    pub fn finish(mut self) -> Result<TableStats> {\n        self.flush_block()?;\n        let index_bytes = self.index.finish();\n        let index_offset = self.offset;\n        self.sink.write_all(&index_bytes)?;\n        self.offset += index_bytes.len() as u64;\n        let mut footer = Vec::with_capacity(32);\n        footer.extend(index_offset.to_le_bytes());\n        footer.extend((index_bytes.len() as u64).to_le_bytes());\n        footer.extend(self.num_entries.to_le_bytes());\n        footer.extend(MAGIC.to_le_bytes());\n        self.sink.write_all(&footer)?;\n        self.offset += 32;\n        self.sink.sync()?;\n        self.finished = true;\n        Ok(TableStats { num_entries, num_blocks, file_size: self.offset })\n    }\n}\n```\n\nNote: index BlockBuilder with restart_interval 1 → every entry is a restart (shared=0). 数据块：块内前缀压缩 + 重启点 + CRC32 校验。\n//!\n//! 块内字节布局（全部小端）：\n//!\n//! ```text\n//! +--------------------------------------------------+\n//! | entry 0 | entry 1 | ... | entry N-1              |  <- 条目区\n//! | restart[0] (u32) ... restart[R-1] (u32)          |  <- 重启点偏移数组（相对块起始）\n//! | num_restarts (u32)                               |\n//! | crc32 (u32)  -- 覆盖此前全部字节                 |\n//! +--------------------------------------------------+\n//!\n//! 单个 entry：\n//!   shared_len   varint  与前一条键的公共前缀长度（重启点处必须为 0）\n//!   unshared_len varint  键剩余部分长度\n//!   value_len    varint\n//!   key_delta    [u8; unshared_len]\n//!   value        [u8; value_len]\n//! ```\n\nuse crate::crc32::crc32;\nuse crate::error::{Error, Result};\nuse crate::varint::{get_uvarint, put_uvarint};\n\n/// 块尾 CRC 字节数。\npub const BLOCK_CRC_SIZE: usize = 4;\n/// 块尾固定部分（重启数组之外）：num_restarts(4) + crc(4)。\nconst BLOCK_FIXED_TRAILER: usize = 8;\n\nfn common_prefix(a: &[u8], b: &[u8]) -> usize {\n    let n = a.len().min(b.len());\n    let mut i = 0;\n    while i < n && a[i] == b[i] {\n        i += 1;\n    }\n    i\n}\n\n/// 块构建器。调用方必须保证按键严格递增顺序添加。\npub struct BlockBuilder {\n    restart_interval: usize,\n    buf: Vec<u8>,\n    restarts: Vec<u32>,\n    counter: usize,\n    last_key: Vec<u8>,\n    num_entries: usize,\n}\n\nimpl BlockBuilder {\n    pub fn new(restart_interval: usize) -> Self {\n        BlockBuilder {\n            restart_interval: restart_interval.max(1),\n            buf: Vec::new(),\n            restarts: vec![0],\n            counter: 0,\n            last_key: Vec::new(),\n            num_entries: 0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn add(&mut self, key: &[u8], value: &[u8]) {\n        let shared = if self.counter < self.restart_interval {\n            common_prefix(&self.last_key, key)\n        } else {\n            // 到达重启点：记录偏移，不再压缩前缀\n            self.restarts.push(self.buf.len() as u32);\n            self.counter = 0;\n            0\n        };\n        put_uvarint(&mut self.buf, shared as u64);\n        put_uvarint(&mut self.buf, (key.len() - shared) as u64);\n        put_uvarint(&mut self.buf, value.len() as u64);\n        self.buf.extend_from_slice(&key[shared..]);\n        self.buf.extend_from_slice(value);\n        self.last_key.clear();\n        self.last_key.extend_from_slice(key);\n        self.counter += 1;\n        self.num_entries += 1;\n    }\n\n    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {\n        self.num_entries == 0\n    }\n\n    pub fn num_entries(&self) -> usize {\n        self.num_entries\n    }\n\n    /// 当前块内最后添加的键（调用方保证非空时使用）。\n    pub fn last_key(&self) -> &[u8] {\n        &self.last_key\n    }\n\n    /// 估算落盘后的块大小（含重启数组与 CRC）。\n    pub fn estimated_size(&self) -> usize {\n        self.buf.len() + self.restarts.len() * 4 + BLOCK_FIXED_TRAILER\n    }\n\n    /// 产出最终块字节（条目 + 重启数组 + 数量 + CRC）。\n    pub fn finish(&self) -> Vec<u8> {\n        let mut out = self.buf.clone();\n        for r in &self.restarts {\n            out.extend_from_slice(&r.to_le_bytes());\n        }\n        out.extend_from_slice(&(self.restarts.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());\n        let crc = crc32(&out);\n        out.extend_from_slice(&crc.to_le_bytes());\n        out\n    }\n\n    pub fn reset(&mut self) {\n        self.buf.clear();\n        self.restarts.clear();\n        self.restarts.push(0);\n        self.counter = 0;\n        self.last_key.clear();\n        self.num_entries = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n/// 只读块视图。构造时校验 CRC 与重启数组边界。\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct BlockReader {\n    data: Vec<u8>,       // 完整块字节（含 CRC）\n    entries_end: usize,  // 条目区结束偏移（即重启数组起始）\n    num_restarts: usize,\n}\n\nimpl BlockReader {\n    /// 解析并校验一个块（`raw` 含 CRC 尾部）。\n    pub fn parse(raw: &[u8]) -> Result<BlockReader> {\n        if raw.len() < BLOCK_FIXED_TRAILER + 4 {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"block too small: {} bytes\",\n                raw.len()\n            )));\n        }\n        let (body, crc_bytes) = raw.split_at(raw.len() - BLOCK_CRC_SIZE);\n        let stored = u32::from_le_bytes(crc_bytes.try_into().unwrap());\n        let actual = crc32(body);\n        if actual != stored {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"block crc mismatch: stored {stored:#010x}, computed {actual:#010x}\"\n            )));\n        }\n        let num = u32::from_le_bytes(body[body.len() - 4..].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;\n        if num == 0 {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(\"block has zero restart points\"));\n        }\n        let restarts_bytes = num\n            .checked_mul(4)\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"restart count overflows\"))?;\n        if body.len() < 4 + restarts_bytes {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"restart array out of bounds: num_restarts={num}, block body={} bytes\",\n                body.len()\n            )));\n        }\n        let entries_end = body.len() - 4 - restarts_bytes;\n        Ok(BlockReader {\n            data: raw.to_vec(),\n            entries_end,\n            num_restarts: num,\n        })\n    }\n\n    pub fn num_restarts(&self) -> usize {\n        self.num_restarts\n    }\n\n    fn restart_offset(&self, i: usize) -> usize {\n        let off = self.entries_end + i * 4;\n        u32::from_le_bytes(self.data[off..off + 4].try_into().unwrap()) as usize\n    }\n\n    /// 解码 `off` 处的条目标头，返回 (shared, unshared, value_len, header_len)。\n    fn decode_header(&self, off: usize) -> Result<(usize, usize, usize, usize)> {\n        let region = &self.data[..self.entries_end];\n        if off >= self.entries_end {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"entry offset {off} out of bounds (entries end at {})\",\n                self.entries_end\n            )));\n        }\n        let (shared, n1) = get_uvarint(&region[off..])\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"bad varint: shared_len\"))?;\n        let (unshared, n2) = get_uvarint(&region[off + n1..])\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"bad varint: unshared_len\"))?;\n        let (vlen, n3) = get_uvarint(&region[off + n1 + n2..])\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"bad varint: value_len\"))?;\n        let hdr = n1 + n2 + n3;\n        let (shared, unshared, vlen) = (shared as usize, unshared as usize, vlen as usize);\n        // 边界检查（防溢出用 checked）\n        let need = unshared\n            .checked_add(vlen)\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"entry length overflow\"))?;\n        if off + hdr + need > self.entries_end {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"entry at {off} overruns entries region (end {})\",\n                self.entries_end\n            )));\n        }\n        Ok((shared, unshared, vlen, hdr))\n    }\n\n    /// 解码 `off` 处的完整条目。`prev_key` 为前一条目的完整键。\n    /// 返回 (key, value, next_offset)。\n    fn decode_entry_at(&self, off: usize, prev_key: &[u8]) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>, usize)> {\n        let (shared, unshared, vlen, hdr) = self.decode_header(off)?;\n        if shared > prev_key.len() {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"entry at {off}: shared prefix {shared} exceeds previous key length {}\",\n                prev_key.len()\n            )));\n        }\n        let key_start = off + hdr;\n        let val_start = key_start + unshared;\n        let next = val_start + vlen;\n        let mut key = Vec::with_capacity(shared + unshared);\n        key.extend_from_slice(&prev_key[..shared]);\n        key.extend_from_slice(&self.data[key_start..val_start]);\n        let value = self.data[val_start..next].to_vec();\n        Ok((key, value, next))\n    }\n\n    /// 解码重启点处的完整键（该处 shared 必须为 0）。\n    fn restart_key(&self, restart_idx: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {\n        let off = self.restart_offset(restart_idx);\n        let (shared, _, _, _) = self.decode_header(off)?;\n        if shared != 0 {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"restart point {restart_idx} at offset {off} has shared_len={shared}, expected 0\"\n            )));\n        }\n        let (key, _, _) = self.decode_entry_at(off, &[])?;\n        Ok(key)\n    }\n\n    /// 从头开始的迭代器。\n    pub fn iter(&self) -> BlockIter {\n        BlockIter {\n            block: self.clone(),\n            next_offset: 0,\n            prev_key: Vec::new(),\n            done: false,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// 定位到第一个 `>= target` 的条目。\n    /// 先对重启点二分，再在重启区间内线性前进。\n    pub fn seek(&self, target: &[u8]) -> Result<BlockIter> {\n        // 二分：找最大的满足 restart_key(i) <= target 的 i\n        let mut lo = 0usize;\n        let mut hi = self.num_restarts - 1;\n        let mut ans = 0usize;\n        while lo <= hi {\n            let mid = (lo + hi) / 2;\n            let k = self.restart_key(mid)?;\n            if k.as_slice() <= target {\n                ans = mid;\n                lo = mid + 1;\n            } else if mid == 0 {\n                break;\n            } else {\n                hi = mid - 1;\n            }\n        }\n        // 从重启点线性前进，直到键 >= target\n        let mut off = self.restart_offset(ans);\n        let mut prev_key: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();\n        while off < self.entries_end {\n            let (key, _, next) = self.decode_entry_at(off, &prev_key)?;\n            if key.as_slice() >= target {\n                return Ok(BlockIter {\n                    block: self.clone(),\n                    next_offset: off,\n                    prev_key,\n                    done: false,\n                });\n            }\n            prev_key = key;\n            off = next;\n        }\n        Ok(BlockIter {\n            block: self.clone(),\n            next_offset: self.entries_end,\n            prev_key,\n            done: true,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// 严格校验块内全部不变量，返回条目数。\n    ///\n    /// 检查项：\n    /// - 每个条目可完整解码，shared_len 不超过前一键长度\n    /// - 键严格递增\n    /// - 条目区被条目恰好占满（无空洞、无越界）\n    /// - 重启数组：首个为 0、严格递增、每个都落在条目边界上\n    /// - 重启点处条目的 shared_len 必须为 0\n    pub fn verify_strict(&self) -> Result<usize> {\n        let mut entry_offsets = Vec::new();\n        let mut off = 0usize;\n        let mut prev_key: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();\n        let mut prev: Option<Vec<u8>> = None;\n        while off < self.entries_end {\n            entry_offsets.push(off);\n            let (key, _, next) = self.decode_entry_at(off, &prev_key)?;\n            if let Some(p) = &prev {\n                if key.as_slice() <= p.as_slice() {\n                    return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                        \"keys not strictly increasing at entry offset {off}\"\n                    )));\n                }\n            }\n            prev_key = key.clone();\n            prev = Some(key);\n            off = next;\n        }\n        // decode_entry_at 的边界检查保证 next <= entries_end；循环退出时必然相等\n        debug_assert_eq!(off, self.entries_end);\n\n        if self.restart_offset(0) != 0 {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"first restart offset is {}, expected 0\",\n                self.restart_offset(0)\n            )));\n        }\n        for i in 1..self.num_restarts {\n            let cur = self.restart_offset(i);\n            let prev_r = self.restart_offset(i - 1);\n            if cur <= prev_r {\n                return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                    \"restart offsets not strictly increasing: restart[{i}]={cur} <= restart[{}]={prev_r}\",\n                    i - 1\n                )));\n            }\n        }\n        for i in 0..self.num_restarts {\n            let r = self.restart_offset(i);\n            match entry_offsets.binary_search(&r) {\n                Ok(_) => {}\n                Err(_) => {\n                    return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                        \"restart offset {r} (restart[{i}]) does not point to an entry start\"\n                    )))\n                }\n            }\n            // 重启点处 shared_len 必须为 0\n            let (shared, _, _, _) = self.decode_header(r)?;\n            if shared != 0 {\n                return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                    \"restart[{i}] at offset {r} has shared_len={shared}, expected 0\"\n                )));\n            }\n        }\n        if entry_offsets.is_empty() && self.num_restarts != 1 {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(\n                \"empty block must have exactly one restart point\",\n            ));\n        }\n        Ok(entry_offsets.len())\n    }\n}\n\n/// 拥有块数据的迭代器，按序产出 (key, value)。\n/// 解码错误以 `Err` 产出一次后迭代结束，不会 panic。\npub struct BlockIter {\n    block: BlockReader,\n    next_offset: usize,\n    prev_key: Vec<u8>,\n    done: bool,\n}\n\nimpl Iterator for BlockIter {\n    type Item = Result<(Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>)>;\n\n    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<Self::Item> {\n        if self.done || self.next_offset >= self.block.entries_end {\n            return None;\n        }\n        match self\n            .block\n            .decode_entry_at(self.next_offset, &self.prev_key)\n        {\n            Ok((key, value, next)) => {\n                self.prev_key = key.clone();\n                self.next_offset = next;\n                Some(Ok((key, value)))\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                self.done = true;\n                Some(Err(e))\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn build(entries: &[(&[u8], &[u8])], restart_interval: usize) -> Vec<u8> {\n        let mut b = 数据块：块内前缀压缩 + 重启点 + CRC32 校验。\n//!\n//! 块内字节布局（全部小端）：\n//!\n//! ```text\n//! +--------------------------------------------------+\n//! | entry 0 | entry 1 | ... | entry N-1              |  <- 条目区\n//! | restart[0] (u32) ... restart[R-1] (u32)          |  <- 重启点偏移数组（相对块起始）\n//! | num_restarts (u32)                               |\n//! | crc32 (u32)  -- 覆盖此前全部字节                 |\n//! +--------------------------------------------------+\n//!\n//! 单个 entry：\n//!   shared_len   varint  与前一条键的公共前缀长度（重启点处必须为 0）\n//!   unshared_len varint  键剩余部分长度\n//!   value_len    varint\n//!   key_delta    [u8; unshared_len]\n//!   value        [u8; value_len]\n//! ```\n\nuse crate::crc32::crc32;\nuse crate::error::{Error, Result};\nuse crate::varint::{get_uvarint, put_uvarint};\n\n/// 块尾 CRC 字节数。\npub const BLOCK_CRC_SIZE: usize = 4;\n/// 块尾固定部分（重启数组之外）：num_restarts(4) + crc(4)。\nconst BLOCK_FIXED_TRAILER: usize = 8;\n\nfn common_prefix(a: &[u8], b: &[u8]) -> usize {\n    let n = a.len().min(b.len());\n    let mut i = 0;\n    while i < n && a[i] == b[i] {\n        i += 1;\n    }\n    i\n}\n\n/// 块构建器。调用方必须保证按键严格递增顺序添加。\npub struct BlockBuilder {\n    restart_interval: usize,\n    buf: Vec<u8>,\n    restarts: Vec<u32>,\n    counter: usize,\n    last_key: Vec<u8>,\n    num_entries: usize,\n}\n\nimpl BlockBuilder {\n    pub fn new(restart_interval: usize) -> Self {\n        BlockBuilder {\n            restart_interval: restart_interval.max(1),\n            buf: Vec::new(),\n            restarts: vec![0],\n            counter: 0,\n            last_key: Vec::new(),\n            num_entries: 0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn add(&mut self, key: &[u8], value: &[u8]) {\n        let shared = if self.counter < self.restart_interval {\n            common_prefix(&self.last_key, key)\n        } else {\n            // 到达重启点：记录偏移，不再压缩前缀\n            self.restarts.push(self.buf.len() as u32);\n            self.counter = 0;\n            0\n        };\n        put_uvarint(&mut self.buf, shared as u64);\n        put_uvarint(&mut self.buf, (key.len() - shared) as u64);\n        put_uvarint(&mut self.buf, value.len() as u64);\n        self.buf.extend_from_slice(&key[shared..]);\n        self.buf.extend_from_slice(value);\n        self.last_key.clear();\n        self.last_key.extend_from_slice(key);\n        self.counter += 1;\n        self.num_entries += 1;\n    }\n\n    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {\n        self.num_entries == 0\n    }\n\n    pub fn num_entries(&self) -> usize {\n        self.num_entries\n    }\n\n    /// 当前块内最后添加的键（调用方保证非空时使用）。\n    pub fn last_key(&self) -> &[u8] {\n        &self.last_key\n    }\n\n    /// 估算落盘后的块大小（含重启数组与 CRC）。\n    pub fn estimated_size(&self) -> usize {\n        self.buf.len() + self.restarts.len() * 4 + BLOCK_FIXED_TRAILER\n    }\n\n    /// 产出最终块字节（条目 + 重启数组 + 数量 + CRC）。\n    pub fn finish(&self) -> Vec<u8> {\n        let mut out = self.buf.clone();\n        for r in &self.restarts {\n            out.extend_from_slice(&r.to_le_bytes());\n        }\n        out.extend_from_slice(&(self.restarts.len() as u32).to_le_bytes());\n        let crc = crc32(&out);\n        out.extend_from_slice(&crc.to_le_bytes());\n        out\n    }\n\n    pub fn reset(&mut self) {\n        self.buf.clear();\n        self.restarts.clear();\n        self.restarts.push(0);\n        self.counter = 0;\n        self.last_key.clear();\n        self.num_entries = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n/// 只读块视图。构造时校验 CRC 与重启数组边界。\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct BlockReader {\n    data: Vec<u8>,       // 完整块字节（含 CRC）\n    entries_end: usize,  // 条目区结束偏移（即重启数组起始）\n    num_restarts: usize,\n}\n\nimpl BlockReader {\n    /// 解析并校验一个块（`raw` 含 CRC 尾部）。\n    pub fn parse(raw: &[u8]) -> Result<BlockReader> {\n        if raw.len() < BLOCK_FIXED_TRAILER + 4 {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"block too small: {} bytes\",\n                raw.len()\n            )));\n        }\n        let (body, crc_bytes) = raw.split_at(raw.len() - BLOCK_CRC_SIZE);\n        let stored = u32::from_le_bytes(crc_bytes.try_into().unwrap());\n        let actual = crc32(body);\n        if actual != stored {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"block crc mismatch: stored {stored:#010x}, computed {actual:#010x}\"\n            )));\n        }\n        let num = u32::from_le_bytes(body[body.len() - 4..].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;\n        if num == 0 {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(\"block has zero restart points\"));\n        }\n        let restarts_bytes = num\n            .checked_mul(4)\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"restart count overflows\"))?;\n        if body.len() < 4 + restarts_bytes {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"restart array out of bounds: num_restarts={num}, block body={} bytes\",\n                body.len()\n            )));\n        }\n        let entries_end = body.len() - 4 - restarts_bytes;\n        Ok(BlockReader {\n            data: raw.to_vec(),\n            entries_end,\n            num_restarts: num,\n        })\n    }\n\n    pub fn num_restarts(&self) -> usize {\n        self.num_restarts\n    }\n\n    fn restart_offset(&self, i: usize) -> usize {\n        let off = self.entries_end + i * 4;\n        u32::from_le_bytes(self.data[off..off + 4].try_into().unwrap()) as usize\n    }\n\n    /// 解码 `off` 处的条目标头，返回 (shared, unshared, value_len, header_len)。\n    fn decode_header(&self, off: usize) -> Result<(usize, usize, usize, usize)> {\n        let region = &self.data[..self.entries_end];\n        if off >= self.entries_end {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"entry offset {off} out of bounds (entries end at {})\",\n                self.entries_end\n            )));\n        }\n        let (shared, n1) = get_uvarint(&region[off..])\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"bad varint: shared_len\"))?;\n        let (unshared, n2) = get_uvarint(&region[off + n1..])\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"bad varint: unshared_len\"))?;\n        let (vlen, n3) = get_uvarint(&region[off + n1 + n2..])\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"bad varint: value_len\"))?;\n        let hdr = n1 + n2 + n3;\n        let (shared, unshared, vlen) = (shared as usize, unshared as usize, vlen as usize);\n        // 边界检查（防溢出用 checked）\n        let need = unshared\n            .checked_add(vlen)\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"entry length overflow\"))?;\n        if off + hdr + need > self.entries_end {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"entry at {off} overruns entries region (end {})\",\n                self.entries_end\n            )));\n        }\n        Ok((shared, unshared, vlen, hdr))\n    }\n\n    /// 解码 `off` 处的完整条目。`prev_key` 为前一条目的完整键。\n    /// 返回 (key, value, next_offset)。\n    fn decode_entry_at(&self, off: usize, prev_key: &[u8]) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>, usize)> {\n        let (shared, unshared, vlen, hdr) = self.decode_header(off)?;\n        if shared > prev_key.len() {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"entry at {off}: shared prefix {shared} exceeds previous key length {}\",\n                prev_key.len()\n            )));\n        }\n        let key_start = off + hdr;\n        let val_start = key_start + unshared;\n        let next = val_start + vlen;\n        let mut key = Vec::with_capacity(shared + unshared);\n        key.extend_from_slice(&prev_key[..shared]);\n        key.extend_from_slice(&self.data[key_start..val_start]);\n        let value = self.data[val_start..next].to_vec();\n        Ok((key, value, next))\n    }\n\n    /// 解码重启点处的完整键（该处 shared 必须为 0）。\n    fn restart_key(&self, restart_idx: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {\n        let off = self.restart_offset(restart_idx);\n        let (shared, _, _, _) = self.decode_header(off)?;\n        if shared != 0 {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"restart point {restart_idx} at offset {off} has shared_len={shared}, expected 0\"\n            )));\n        }\n        let (key, _, _) = self.decode_entry_at(off, &[])?;\n        Ok(key)\n    }\n\n    /// 从头开始的迭代器。\n    pub fn iter(&self) -> BlockIter {\n        BlockIter {\n            block: self.clone(),\n            next_offset: 0,\n            prev_key: Vec::new(),\n            done: false,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// 定位到第一个 `>= target` 的条目。\n    /// 先对重启点二分，再在重启区间内线性前进。\n    pub fn seek(&self, target: &[u8]) -> Result<BlockIter> {\n        // 二分：找最大的满足 restart_key(i) <= target 的 i\n        let mut lo = 0usize;\n        let mut hi = self.num_restarts - 1;\n        let mut ans = 0usize;\n        while lo <= hi {\n            let mid = (lo + hi) / 2;\n            let k = self.restart_key(mid)?;\n            if k.as_slice() <= target {\n                ans = mid;\n                lo = mid + 1;\n            } else if mid == 0 {\n                break;\n            } else {\n                hi = mid - 1;\n            }\n        }\n        // 从重启点线性前进，直到键 >= target\n        let mut off = self.restart_offset(ans);\n        let mut prev_key: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();\n        while off < self.entries_end {\n            let (key, _, next) = self.decode_entry_at(off, &prev_key)?;\n            if key.as_slice() >= target {\n                return Ok(BlockIter {\n                    block: self.clone(),\n                    next_offset: off,\n                    prev_key,\n                    done: false,\n                });\n            }\n            prev_key = key;\n            off = next;\n        }\n        Ok(BlockIter {\n            block: self.clone(),\n            next_offset: self.entries_end,\n            prev_key,\n            done: true,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// 严格校验块内全部不变量，返回条目数。\n    ///\n    /// 检查项：\n    /// - 每个条目可完整解码，shared_len 不超过前一键长度\n    /// - 键严格递增\n    /// - 条目区被条目恰好占满（无空洞、无越界）\n    /// - 重启数组：首个为 0、严格递增、每个都落在条目边界上\n    /// - 重启点处条目的 shared_len 必须为 0\n    pub fn verify_strict(&self) -> Result<usize> {\n        let mut entry_offsets = Vec::new();\n        let mut off = 0usize;\n        let mut prev_key: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();\n        let mut prev: Option<Vec<u8>> = None;\n        while off < self.entries_end {\n            entry_offsets.push(off);\n            let (key, _, next) = self.decode_entry_at(off, &prev_key)?;\n            if let Some(p) = &prev {\n                if key.as_slice() <= p.as_slice() {\n                    return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                        \"keys not      }\n        // decode_entry_at 的边界检查保证 next <= entries_end；循环退出时必然相等\n        debug_assert_eq!(off, self.entries_end);\n\n        if self.restart_offset(0) != 0 {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"first restart offset is {}, expected 0\",\n                self.restart_offset(0)\n            )));\n        }\n        for i in 1..self.num_restarts {\n            let cur = self.restart_offset(i);\n            let prev_r = self.restart_offset(i - 1);\n            if cur <= prev_r {\n                return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                    \"restart offsets not strictly increasing: restart[{i}]={cur} <= restart[{}]={prev_r}\",\n                    i - 1\n                )));\n            }\n        }\n        for i in 0..self.num_restarts {\n            let r = self.restart_offset(i);\n            match entry_offsets.binary_search(&r) {\n                Ok(_) => {}\n                Err(_) => {\n                    return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                        \"restart offset {r} (restart[{i}]) does not point to an entry start\"\n                    )))\n                }\n            }\n            // 重启点处 shared_len 必须为 0\n            let (shared, _, _, _) = self.decode_header(r)?;\n            if shared != 0 {\n                return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                    \"restart[{i}] at offset {r} has shared_len={shared}, expected 0\"\n                )));\n            }\n        }\n        if entry_offsets.is_empty() && self.num_restarts != 1 {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(\n                \"empty block must have exactly one restart point\",\n            ));\n        }\n        Ok(entry_offsets.len())\n    }\n}\n\n/// 拥有块数据的迭代器，按序产出 (key, value)。\n/// 解码错误以 `Err` 产出一次后迭代结束，不会 panic。\npub struct BlockIter {\n    block: BlockReader,\n    next_offset: usize,\n    prev_key: Vec<u8>,\n    done: bool,\n}\n\nimpl Iterator for BlockIter {\n    type Item = Result<(Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>)>;\n\n    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<Self::Item> {\n        if self.done || self.next_offset >= self.block.entries_end {\n            return None;\n        }\n        match self\n            .block\n            .decode_entry_at(self.next_offset, &self.prev_key)\n        {\n            Ok((key, value, next)) => {\n                self.prev_key = key.clone();\n                self.next_offset = next;\n                Some(Ok((key, value)))\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                self.done = true;\n                Some(Err(e))\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    fn build(entries: &[(&[u8], &[u8])], restart_interval: usize) -> Vec<u8> {\n        let mut b = n 次 `write_all` 只写入前 k 字节却报告成功（撕裂写）；\n/// - `fail_sync(true)`：`sync` 返回 I/O 错误（模拟 fsync 失败）。\npub struct FaultyWriter<W: WriteSink> {\n    inner: W,\n    call: usize,\n    pub fail_on_call:       inner,\n            call: 0,\n            fail_on_call: None,\n            partial_on_call: None,\n            fail_sync: false,\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn into_inner(self) -> W {\n        self.inner\n    }\n}\n\nimpl<W: WriteSink> WriteSink for FaultyWriter<W> {\n    fn write_all(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {\n        let call = self.call;\n        self.call += 1;\n        if self.fail_on_call == Some(call) {\n            return Err(Error::Io(std::io::Error::new(\n                std::io::ErrorKind::Other,\n                format!(\"injected write failure on call num_entries: u64,\n    file_size: u64,\n    index_offset: u64,\n    index_size: u64,\n}\n\nimpl<R: ReadAt> TableReader<R> {\n    /// 打开并做基本校验：长度、魔数、索引边界、索引块 CRC、索引条目可解码。\n    pub fn open(r: R) -> let (size, n2) = get_uvarint(&v[n1..])\n                .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"index value: bad size varint\"))?;\n            if n1 + n2 != v.len() {\n                return {"parentUuid":"c6c64da7-dcff-4e98-a55e-df8fdb4d44ed","isSidechain":false,"message":{"content":[{"id":"call_36191f50a8944636adf72018","input":{"file_path":"/home/admin/Downloads/biaozhul/opp8/a/README.md","content":"# sst — 前缀压缩有序表（只读 SSTable 风格存储）\n\n纯后端 Rust 项目，零外部依赖。提供：\n\n- 文件支持的只读有序表存储库（`src/` 库 crate）\n- 可注入 I/O 层（`ReadAt` / `WriteSink` trait），测试可模拟写失败、sync 失败、撕裂写、读失败\n- 本地 HTTP 验证入口与 CLI（`sst` 二进制）\n- 严格格式校验（`verify`）\n\n## 构建与测试\n\n```bash\ncargo build --release\ncargo test\n```\n\n## 磁盘格式\n\n全部整数为小端。文件布局：\n\n```\n+----------------+  <-- 偏移 0\n| data block 0   |\n| data block 1   |\n| ...            |\n| index block    |  <-- footer.index_offset\n| footer (32B)   |  <-- file_len - 32\n+----------------+\n```\n\n### 数据块 / 索引块（同一格式）\n\n```\n| entry 0 | entry 1 | ... | entry N-1            条目区\n| restart[0] u32 ... restart[R-1] u32            重启点偏移数组（相对块起始）\n| num_restarts u32\n| crc32 u32                                      覆盖此前全部字节（IEEE 多项式）\n```\n\n单个 entry：\n\n```\nshared_len   varint   与前一条键的公共前缀长度（重启点处必须为 0）\nunshared_len varint   键剩余部分长度\nvalue_len    varint\nkey_delta    [u8; unshared_len]\nvalue        [u8; value_len]\n```\n\n每 `restart_interval` 个条目设一个重启点（shared_len = 0，键完整存储），\n点查时先对重启点二分、再在重启区间内线性解码。\n\n### 索引块（稀疏索引）\n\n索引块本身也是上述块格式（restart_interval = 1）。每个条目：\n\n- 键：对应数据块的**最后一个键**（分隔键）\n- 值：`varint(block_offset) ++ varint(block_size)`（block_size 含 CRC）\n\n点查时在索引中找第一个 `分隔键 >= 目标键` 的块，只读这一块。\n\n### 页脚（定长 32 字节，位于文件末尾）\n\n```\n| index_offset u64 | index_size u64 | num_entries u64 | magic u64 |\n```\n\nmagic = `0x5353_5442_4C45_3031`（\"SSTBLE01\"）。\n\n## 同步边界\n\n- `TableWriter::finish` 依次写入：最后一个数据块 → 索引块 → 页脚，最后调用\n  `WriteSink::sync`（文件实现为 `fsync`/`sync_data`）。**只有 sync 成功返回，\n  表才被视为完整持久。**\n- `write_all` 只保证字节进入内核缓冲；进程崩溃时未 sync 的字节可能丢失，\n  此时文件尾部不完整，打开时会因魔数/索引边界校验失败而被拒绝（见\n  `tests/fault_injection.rs` 的撕裂写用例）。\n- 表构建完成后只读；不支持原地修改。\n\n## 严格校验（verify）\n\n`TableReader::verify` / `sst verify` / `GET /verify` 做全量检查：\n\n- 页脚魔数、索引边界、索引块恰好结束于页脚起始\n- 每个块：CRC32、条目可完整解码、shared_len 不超过前一键长度、键严格递增、\n  条目区无空洞\n- 重启数组：首项为 0、严格递增、每项落在条目边界、重启点处 shared_len 为 0\n- 数据块从偏移 0 起连续排布、跨块键序递增、索引分隔键等于块内最后键\n- 条目总数与页脚一致\n\n## 可注入 I/O 层\n\n```rust\npub trait ReadAt: Send + Sync { fn read_at(&self, buf: &mut [u8], offset: u64) -> Result<()>; fn len(&self) -> Result<u64>; }\npub trait WriteSink: Send { fn write_all(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<()>; fn sync(&mut self) -> Result<()>; }\n```\n\n实现：`FileReader` / `FileWriter`（文件）、`MemStorage`（内存，测试/演示）、\n`FaultyWriter`（故障注入：指定第 N 次写失败、撕裂写只落前 k 字节、sync 失败）。\n\n## CLI\n\n```bash\n# 生成 5000 条随机 kv（hexkey=hexvalue 行），构建表（键自动排序，重复键报错）\n./target/release/sst gen --n 5000 --seed 7 --prefix 757365723a \\\n  | ./target/release/sst build --out /tmp/demo.sst --block-size 512 --restart-interval 8\n\n./target/release/sst verify --table /tmp/demo.sst\n./target/release/sst get    --table /tmp/demo.sst --key 757365723a00\n./target/release/sst scan   --table /tmp/demo.sst --start 7573 --end 7574 --limit 10\n./target/release/sst serve  --table /tmp/demo.sst --addr 127.0.0.1:18080\n```\n\n键值在 CLI/HTTP 接口中一律用十六进制表示（空键 = 空串）。\n\n## HTTP 验证入口\n\n`sst serve --table FILE --addr 127.0.0.1:18080`，仅 GET，JSON 响应：\n\n| 路径 | 说明 |\n|---|---|\n| `GET /health` | 存活探针 |\n| `GET /stats` | 条目数、块数、文件大小 |\n| `GET /get?key=<hex>` | 点查 |\n| `GET /scan?start=<hex>&end=<hex>&limit=N` | 范围扫描 `[start, end)`，end 可省略 |\n| `GET /verify` | 严格校验，失败返回 HTTP 500 + 错误详情 |\n\n请求样例见 [examples/requests.sh](examples/requests.sh)。\n\n## 自动化测试（36 个，全部通过）\n\n- `src/` 单元测试 ×13：varint 边界、CRC32 标准向量、hex、块往返/seek/空块/损坏重启点/非法 shared_len\n- `tests/random_vs_model.rs` ×7：随机键对照 `BTreeMap`（点查命中/未命中、全表扫描、200 组随机范围扫描），\n  覆盖空键空值、300 字节长公共前缀、跨块扫描（128B 小块）、restart_interval=1、超大值跨块、空表、乱序/重复键拒绝\n- `tests/corruption.rs` ×10：损坏重启偏移（越界/指向条目中间）、CRC 不匹配、非法 shared_len、\n  截断、坏魔数、索引越界、条目数不符、索引分隔键不符 —— 全部要求报错而非 panic/错数据\n- `tests/fault_injection.rs` ×5：sync 失败、逐写调用点失败注入、撕裂写（页脚/数据块）、读失败\n- `tests/http_smoke.rs` ×1：真实启动 `sst serve`，HTTP 请求验证全部端点与错误码\n\n## 实际运行记录（2026-09-23，rustc 1.90.0，Linux x86_64）\n\n```\n$ cargo test\ntest result: ok. 13 passed; unshared_len]\nvalue        [u8; value_len]\n```\n\n每 `restart_interval` 个条目设一个重启点（shared_len = 0，键完整存储），\n点查时先对重启点二分、再在重启区间内线性解码。\n\n### 索引块（稀疏索引）\n\n索引块本身也是上述块格式（restart_interval = 1）。每个条目：\n\n- 键：对应数据块的**最后一个键**（分隔键）\n- 值：`varint(block_offset) ++ varint(block_size)`（block_size 含 CRC）\n\n点查时在索引中找第一个 `分隔键 >= 目标键` 的块，只读这一块。\n\n### 页脚（定长 32 字节，位于文件末尾）\n\n```\n| index_offset u64 | index_size u64 | num_entries u64 | magic u64 |\n```\n\nmagic = `0x5353_5442_4C45_3031`（\"SSTBLE01\"）。\n\n## 同步边界\n\n- `TableWriter::finish` 依次写入：最后一个数据块 → 索引块 → 页脚，最后调用\n  `WriteSink::sync`（文件实现为 `fsync`/`sync_data`）。**只有 sync 成功返回，\n  表才被视为完整持久。**\n- `write_all` 只保证字节进入内核缓冲；进程崩溃时未 sync 的字节可能丢失，\n  此时文件尾部不完整，打开时会因魔数/索引边界校验失败而被拒绝（见\n  `tests/fault_injection.rs` 的撕裂写用例）。\n- 表构建完成后只读；不支持原地修改。\n\n## 严格校验（verify）\n\n`TableReader::verify` / `sst verify` / `GET /verify` 做全量检查：\n\n- 页脚魔数、索引边界、索引块恰好结束于页脚起始\n- 每个块：CRC32、条目可完整解码、shared_len 不超过前一键长度、键严格递增、\n  条目区无空洞\n- 重启数组：首项为 0、严格递增、每项落在条目边界、重启点处 shared_len 为 0\n- 数据块从偏移 0 起连续排布、跨块键序递增、索引分隔键等于块内最后键\n- 条目总数与页脚一致\n\n## 可注入 I/O 层\n\n```rust\npub trait ReadAt: Send + Sync { fn read_at(&self, buf: &mut [u8], offset: u64) -> Result<()>; fn len(&self) -> Result<u64>; }\npub trait WriteSink: Send { fn write_all(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<()>; fn sync(&mut self) -> Result<()>; }\n```\n\n实现：`FileReader` / `FileWriter`（文件）、`MemStorage`（内存，测试/演示）、\n`FaultyWriter`（故障注入：指定第 N 次写失败、撕裂写只落前 k 字节、sync 失败）。\n\n## CLI\n\n```bash\n# 生成 5000 条随机 kv（hexkey=hexvalue 行），构建表（键自动排序，重复键报错）\n./target/release/sst gen --n 5000 --seed 7 --prefix 757365723a \\\n  | ./target/release/sst build --out /tmp/demo.sst --block-size 512 --restart-interval 8\n\n./target/release/sst verify --table /tmp/demo.sst\n./target/release/sst get    --table /tmp/demo.sst --key 757365723a00\n./target/release/sst scan   --table /tmp/demo.sst --start 7573 --end 7574 --limit 10\n./target/release/sst serve  --table /tmp/demo.sst --addr 127.0.0.1:18080\n```\n\n键值在 CLI/HTTP 接口中一律用十六进制表示（空键 = 空串）。\n\n## HTTP 验证入口\n\n`sst serve --table FILE --addr 127.0.0.1:18080`，仅 GET，JSON 响应：\n\n| 路径 | 说明 |\n|---|---|\n| `GET /health` | 存活探针 |\n| `GET /stats` | 条目数、块数、文件大小 |\n| `GET /get?key=<hex>` | 点查 |\n| `GET /scan?start=<hex>&end=<hex>&limit=N` | 范围扫描 `[start, end)`，end 可省略 |\n| `GET /verify` | 严格校验，失败返回 HTTP 500 + 错误详情 |\n\n请求样例见 [examples/requests.sh](examples/requests.sh)。\n\n## 自动化测试（36 个，全部通过）\n\n- `src/` 单元测试 ×13：varint 边界、CRC32 标准向量、hex、块往返/seek/空块/损坏重启点/非法 shared_len\n- `tests/random_vs_model.rs` ×7：随机键对照 `BTreeMap`（点查命中/未命中、全表扫描、200 组随机范围扫描），\n  覆盖空键空值、300 字节长公共前缀、跨块扫描（128B 小块）、restart_interval=1、超大值跨块、空表、乱序/重复键拒绝\n- `tests/corruption.rs` ×10：损坏重启偏移（越界/指向条目中间）、CRC 不匹配、非法 shared_len、\n  截断、坏魔数、索引越界、条目数不符、索引分隔键不符 —— 全部要求报错而非 panic/错数据\n- `tests/fault_injection.rs` ×5：sync 失败、逐写调用点失败注入、撕裂写（页脚/数据块）、读失败\n- `tests/http_smoke.rs` ×1：真实启动 `sst serve`，HTTP 请求验证全部端点与错误码\n\n## 实际运行记录（2026-09-23，rustc 1.90.0，Linux x86_64）\n\n```\n$ cargo test\ntest result: ok. 13 passed; 线程安全的内存“文件”。克隆句柄指向同一份数据。\n#[derive(Clone, Default)]\npub struct MemStorage {\n    data: Arc<Mutex<Vec<u8>>>,\n}\n\nimpl MemStorage {\n    pub fn new() -> MemStorage {\n        MemStorage::default()\n    }\n\n    /// 当前全部字节快照。\n    pub fn bytes(&self) -> Vec<u8> {\n        self.data.lock().unwrap().clone()\n    }\n\n    /// 直接修改底层字节（测试用来注入损坏）。\n    pub fn mutate(&self, f: impl FnOnce(&mut Vec<u8>)) {\n        let mut g = self.data.lock().unwrap();\n        f(&mut g);\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ReadAt for MemStorage {\n    fn read_at(&self, buf: &mut [u8], offset: u64) -> Result<()> {\n        let g = self.data.lock().unwrap();\n        let start = offset as usize;\n        let end = start\n            .checked_add(buf.len())\n            .ok_or_else(|| Error::corruption(\"read range overflows\"))?;\n        if end > g.len() {\n            return Err(Error::corruption(format!(\n                \"read out of bounds: [{offset}, {end}) but file is {} bytes\",\n                g.len()\n            )));\n        }\n        buf.copy_from_slice(&g[start..end]);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n    fn len(&self) -> Result<u64> {\n        Ok(self.data.lock().unwrap().len() as u64)\n self) -> Result<()> {\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// 故障注入写包装器\n// n 次 `write_all` 只写入前 k 字节却报告成功（撕裂写）；\n/// - `fail_sync(true)`：`sync` 返回 I/O 错误（模拟 fsync 失败）。\npub struct FaultyWriter<W: WriteSink> {\n    inner: W,\n    call: usize,\n    pub fail_on_call: Option<usize>,\n    pub partial_on_call: Option<(usize, usize)>,\n    pub fail_sync: bool,\n}\n\nimpl<W: WriteSink> FaultyWriter<W> {\n    pub fn new(inner: W) -> Self {\n        FaultyWriter {\n            inner,\n            call: 0,\n            fail_on_call: None,\n            partial_on_call: None,\n            fail_sync: false,\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn into_inner(self) -> W {\n        self.inner\n    }\n}\n\nimpl<W: WriteSink> WriteSink for FaultyWriter<W> {\n    fn write_all(&mut self, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {\n        let call = self.call;\n        self.call += 1;\n        if self.fail_on_call == Some(call) {\n            return Err(Error::Io(std::io::Error::other(\n                format!(\"injected write failure on call {call}\"),\n            )));\n        }\n        if let Some((n, k)) = self.partial_on_call {\n            if n == call {\n                let k = k.min(buf.len());\n                // 撕裂写：只落前 k 字节，但对上层报告成功\n                return self.inner.write_all(&buf[..k]);\n            }\n        }\n        self.inner.write_all(buf)\n    }\n\n    fn sync(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n        if self.fail_sync {\n            return Err(Error::Io(std::io::Error::other(\n                \"injected sync failure\",\n            )));\n        }\n        self.inner.sync()\n
